                               PROC             {R0-R7}
  375 000000FC B5F8            PUSH             {R3-R7, LR}
  376 000000FE         ;   R4 : addend store / sum
  377 000000FE         ;   R5 : addend store
  378 000000FE         ;   R6 : hold APSR

  380 000000FE 0007            MOVS             R7, R0

  383 00000102 1C00            ADDS             R0, R0, #0

  385 00000108         aimu_loop
  386 00000108 1F1B            SUBS             R3, R3, #4
  387 0000010A 58C8            LDR              R0, [R1, R3]

  389 00000110 0004            MOVS             R4, R0
  390 00000112 58D0            LDR              R0, [R2, R3]

  392 00000118 0005            MOVS             R5, R0

  396 00000120 416C            ADCS             R4, R4, R5
  397 00000122         ;  Store APSR flags
  398 00000122 F3EF 8600       MRS              R6, APSR
